Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the biophysics space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 56 and the convergence rate is O(422/n^5).

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_56(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 56. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^422(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the biophysics structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 319. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 148 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on biophysics structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 319. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 148. QED.

By the optimality condition framework applied to biophysics structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 152/250 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.6 (Bound Derivation)

Statement: Every bound derivation deformation of the biophysics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 501. The moduli space has dimension at most 788 with 4 connected components.

For the inductive step, we decompose the biophysics structure using the bound derivation theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 788.
